## part0015

**[1]**

On a beach on the outskirts of camp one, ~~John~~ **Jane** Shepard tapped into ~~his~~ **her** biotics, then hurled a fist-sized piece of rubble into the air. <em>I’ll get the next one. ~~</em>He~~ **</em>She** took the rifle

~~He~~ **She** lowered the rifle, then spotted the piece of rubble falling into the ocean.

**[17]**

In ~~his~~ **her** prime, ~~he~~ **she** had graduated from the N7 program, as one of the best marksmen, able to land perfect headshots on moving targets nearly half a kilometer away. Now, ~~he~~ **she** had the marksmanship of a conscript barely out of boot camp.

**[20]**

~~He~~ **She** stirred, then looked at Garrus.

**[22]**

**[25]**

“Thanks,” ~~he~~ **she** said, taking them. Immediately, ~~he~~ **she** cracked one open, then took a sip. ~~He~~ **She** reclined into the lawn chair and sighed.
